  • ⑧ In-vivo efficacy: model, n, controls (isotype IgG), endpoint, conclusion + limit.⑧ 体内药效：模型、n、同型对照、终点、结论+局限。
  • ⑨ Pros/cons vs another drug: quantified advantages first, then 1–2 hypothesised drawbacks, then a verdict sentence.⑨ 利弊比较：先量化优点，再 1–2 个假设性缺点，最后一句结论。
讲解

这 9 类覆盖了 4 年 24 个小问（2025-26 OZR、2024-25 nirsevimab、2023-24 Bs4Ab-VA、早期 ranibizumab）。药和数据每年换，问法不变。每准备一个新药，只要问自己：“它属于这 9 类中的哪几类？”

各讲对应：①② → L1（杂交瘤、嵌合 / 人源化、细胞株与宿主）；③ → L1（mAb 工业生产流程）；④⑤ → L8（SPR 等）与 L1；⑥⑦ → L1（IgG 的功能、作用机制、FcRn、Fc 改造）；⑧⑨ → 利弊比较的答题模板。

A.3Fay：7 类固定问法Fay: the 7 recurring question types

Fay 的旧材料只有两样：早期真题里的 Exercise 2（miR-181c，与今年练习 1 逐字相同）和今年的两个练习，见附录 B。

Fay 的 7 类固定问法★★★Fay 练课 11–10Fay's recurring question types
答案骨架 · Answer outline
  • ① Nature/structure and mechanism of miRNA (1–2 pts).① miRNA 的本质/结构与作用机制。
  • ② Explain the controls (NC/scramble, inhibitor/antagomir, vehicle…).② 解释对照。
  • ③ Describe and analyze a figure (proliferation curve, luciferase assay, apoptosis bar chart, tumor volume).③ 描述并分析图。
  • ④ Stability strategies (chemical modification, carriers).④ 稳定性策略。
  • ⑤ Delivery and how to measure delivery at the target site.⑤ 递送及如何测量到达靶位。
  • ⑥ siRNA vs miRNA differences.⑥ siRNA 与 miRNA 的区别。
  • ⑦ What control is missing? (e.g. LNP with irrelevant mRNA).⑦ 缺什么对照？
讲解

Fay 要求：“describe, don't write essays”，每小问约 6 行。图题模板（五步）：画了什么 → 趋势+数字 → 各对照作用 → 一句结论 → 一个局限。

附 B

练习题与往年真题Exercises and past papers, with answers

题干为英文原文图表未收录答案点开查看

题干是原卷的英文文字，图和部分表格没有收录；读图得到的数值是近似值，请对照原图。参考答案是学生整理的，不是官方答案。

B.1Fay 练习 1：miR-181c / MAP4K4Exercise 1 · miRNA（与旧卷 Exercise 2 逐字相同）

2026–27 练习课上带做的题；同一道题也出现在早期真题里（见 B.7 的 Exercise 2）。8 个小问，共 10 分。

题干 · Question paper（英文原文，图表未收录）

Exercise 1 A team of Chinese scientists has found that, compare to non-cancer cells, breast cancer cells (MDA-MB-231) express a low level of a specific micro-RNA named miR-181c.The scientists believe that miR-181c regulates the expression of Mitogen-activated protein 4 kinase (MAP4K4).

1) Please briefly describe the nature/structure (1pt) and the mechanism of action (1 pt) of microRNAs.

In a first set of experiments, the scientists incubated MDA-MB-231 cells with either a miR-181c mimics, a miRNA with a scramble sequence (NC), or an anti-microRNA-181c antagomir sequence (miR-181c inhibitor). They then followed the growth of the cells by optical measurement at 492nm.

Figure 2 Effect of miR-181c on proliferation in MDA-MB-231

2) Please explain the 2 controls used in the experiment (1 pts).

3) Please briefly describe and analyze Figure 2 (1 pt). In a second set of experiments, the scientists produced a plasmid containing the gene coding for a fusion protein MAP4K4-Luciferin using either the natural sequence of MAP4K4 or a mutated one. Using bioinformatics analysis, the scientists predicted the binding (or not), of miR-181c on the mRNA of wild MAP4K4 and mutated MAP4K4 (Fig 2A). The scientists transfected MDA-MB cells with the plasmid, and then treated the cells with either miR-181c mimic or scrambled control while measuring the subsequent luciferase activity.

Figure 3 A) sequence of the possible complementary site between miR-181c and wild or mutated MAP4K4. B) Luciferase activity after treatment

4) Please briefly describe and analyze Figure 3 (1 pt).

Following those results, the scientist decided to test the miR-181c mimics in vivo. They planned to deliver them by intravenous injection in a cancer mouse model and follow their distribution.

5) Please explain two strategies the scientists could use to improve the stability of the micro-RNA in vivo. (2 pts).

6) Please describe a strategy the scientists could use to measure the delivery of the micro-RNAs at the tumor site? (1 pt). As MAP4K4 seems to be an interesting target, the scientist then decided to test the effect of a SiRNA targeting MAP4K4 (SiR-MAP4K4).

7) What are the main differences between a siRNA and a micro-RNA? (1 pt).

To quickly test their hypothesis, the scientists incubated MDA-MB-231 cells with either SiR- MAP4K4, or a scramble control (SiR-NC). They then measure the cell mortality (apoptosis rate) after 24h.

Figure 4 Apoptosis rate of MDA-MB-231 after treatment

8) Please briefly describe and analyze Figure 4 (1 pt).

Exercise 2

Ex.1 Q1：miRNA 的本质/结构（1 分）与作用机制（1 分）★★★FayEx.1 Q1
参考答案 · Answer
  • Nature: 20–25 nt endogenous non-coding single-stranded RNA, from a hairpin precursor processed by Drosha/Dicer; loaded into RISC with Argonaute.本质：20–25 nt 的内源非编码单链 RNA，来自发夹前体（Drosha/Dicer 加工），装入 RISC（Argonaute）。
  • Mechanism: seed (nt 2–8) pairs imperfectly with 3′UTR of target mRNA → inhibition of translation and/or deadenylation/decapping → mRNA degradation. One miRNA, many targets.机制：种子序列与靶 mRNA 3′UTR 不完全配对 → 抑制翻译和/或去腺苷酸化/脱帽 → mRNA 降解；一个 miRNA 多个靶点。
讲解

见第 4 讲 miRNA 的本质/结构是什么、miRNA 的作用机制。

Ex.1 Q2：解释两个对照（NC 与 inhibitor）★★★FayEx.1 Q2
参考答案 · Answer
  • NC (scramble mimic): same chemistry, no target → baseline, subtracts non-specific effect of transfecting an oligo.NC：化学相同、无靶点 → 基线，扣除转染的非特异效应。
  • Inhibitor (antagomir): reverse experiment — mimic ↓ proliferation and inhibitor ↑ it shows the effect comes from miR-181c itself.抑制剂：反向实验——mimic 抑制、inhibitor 促进增殖，证明效应来自 miR-181c。
讲解

见 miRNA 实验中的对照：每个对。

Ex.1 Q3：图 2——增殖曲线（OD492，24–120 h）★★★FayEx.1 Q3 Fig. 2
参考答案 · Answer
  • Plotted: cell number (OD492) vs time for NC, mimic, inhibitor in MDA-MB-231.画的是：NC、mimic、inhibitor 三组 MDA-MB-231 的细胞数（OD492）随时间变化。
  • 120 h: mimic ≈0.23 vs NC ≈0.42 (***) ; inhibitor ≈0.52 (***) above NC; difference clear after 72 h.120 h：mimic 约 0.23 vs NC 约 0.42（***）；inhibitor 约 0.52（***）高于 NC；72 h 后差异明显。
  • Conclusion: miR-181c inhibits proliferation; its low endogenous level favors tumor growth. Limit: one cell line, in vitro, proliferation only.结论：miR-181c 抑制增殖；内源低表达利于肿瘤生长。局限：单一细胞系、体外、仅增殖。
讲解

读图数字为近似值，请对照原图。

Ex.1 Q4：图 3——荧光素酶报告基因（WT vs 突变 MAP4K4）★★★FayEx.1 Q4 Fig. 3
参考答案 · Answer
  • MAP4K4(3′UTR)-luciferase WT or mutated at the predicted miR-181c site; transfected cells treated with mimic or NC.MAP4K4-荧光素酶（WT 或预测位点突变）；转染后加 mimic 或 NC。
  • WT: mimic ↓ activity to ≈0.4 (***, NC = 1). Mutant: ≈1.0 (no change).WT：mimic 使活性降到约 0.4（***）；突变：约 1.0 无变化。
  • → miR-181c binds that site in MAP4K4 3′UTR = direct target. (Exam typo: luciferin → luciferase.)→ miR-181c 结合 MAP4K4 3′UTR 该位点 = 直接靶点。（题目笔误：luciferin 应为 luciferase。）
讲解

见 miRNA 实验中的对照：每个对、为什么要用核酸药物。

Ex.1 Q5–Q7：稳定性策略、测量递送、siRNA vs miRNA★★★FayEx.1 Q5–Q7
参考答案 · Answer
  • Q5 two stability strategies: (1) chemical modification (2′-OMe, 2′-F, LNA, phosphorothioate; cholesterol/GalNAc conjugation); (2) carrier (LNP/liposome/polymer NP/EV, PEGylation).Q5 两种稳定性策略：化学修饰；载体包裹/PEG 化。
  • Q6 measure delivery at the tumor: labelled (Cy5) miRNA/carrier + in vivo imaging; or qRT-PCR of the miRNA in tumor extracts (mimic vs NC groups); or FISH.Q6 测肿瘤处递送：荧光标记 + 成像；肿瘤匀浆 qRT-PCR（mimic vs NC）；FISH。
  • Q7 siRNA: exogenous duplex, perfect pairing, Ago2 cleaves, ~1 target. miRNA: endogenous, seed pairing, translational repression/decay, many targets.Q7 siRNA：外源双链、完全配对、Ago2 切割、基本 1 个靶点；miRNA：内源、种子配对、翻译抑制/降解、多靶点。
讲解

见 siRNA 与 miRNA 的主、如何提高 miRNA/RNA 药。

Ex.1 Q8：图 4——siR-MAP4K4 敲低后的凋亡率★★★FayEx.1 Q8 Fig. 4
参考答案 · Answer
  • Apoptosis ≈6% (siR-NC) → ≈11.5% (siR-MAP4K4) (**), ~2×.凋亡率约 6%（siR-NC）→ 约 11.5%（siR-MAP4K4）（**），约 2 倍。
  • MAP4K4 knockdown promotes apoptosis → supports MAP4K4 as a functional target of miR-181c.敲低 MAP4K4 促进凋亡 → 支持 MAP4K4 是 miR-181c 的功能靶点。
  • But absolute apoptosis is low; need knockdown validation (WB/qPCR), proliferation/colony assays, rescue and in vivo experiments.但绝对值低；需验证敲低效率（WB/qPCR）、增殖/集落实验、回补实验、体内实验。

题干 · Question paper（英文原文，图表未收录）

In the following experiments the scientist studied antitumoral efficacy of 3 nanoparticles (LNPs) encapsulating mRNA coding for either GMSCF (DAL4-LNP-GM- CSF), IL-27 (DAL4-LNP-IL-27) or IL-12 (DAL4-LNP-IL-27). In this study they treated B16F10 tumour bearing mice with the LNPs, they then measured the size of the tumour over a period of 22 days and obtained the following results:

Figure 1 B16F10 tumor size after treatment of single cytokine mRNA in DAL4-LNP (n = 6). Data are presented as the mean ± S.E.M. Statistical significance was analyzed using two-way ANOVA with repeated measurements.. *P < 0.05;**P < 0.01; ***P < 0.001.

1) Please briefly describe and analyze Figure 1 (1pt).

B.3Turbica 练习：anti-TNF 与 infliximab 质控Exercise · anti-TNF mAbs and quality control

题干 · Question paper（英文原文，图表未收录）

Exercices Chronic inflammatory diseases such as rheumatoid arthritis, ankylosing spondylitis, systemic lupus erythematosus, psoriasis, Crohn's disease and multiple sclerosis all involve pro-inflammatory cytokines. TNF-α is a prime therapeutic target for the development of monoclonal antibodies (mAbs). This cytokine is indeed overproduced during the natural history of these diseases, either in a membrane form, expressed on the surface of the various producing cells, or in soluble form, secreted by these same cells. The direct and deleterious involvement of TNF-α has been clearly demonstrated in these diseases. Several formats of anti- TNF-α mAbs have been developed since the 1990s, the main molecules of which are shown in Figure 1.

Figure 1.

Question: Describe the particularities of each of these molecules, as well as their associated advantage for their functionality (= activity(ies) of the molecule). What differences and similarities can you cite in the production and purification processes used for these molecules?

mAb 纯化流程每一步的作用（课件图 2）★★★Turbica 练习Anti-TNF exercise: purification steps
参考答案 · Answer
  • Clarified supernatant (1–5 g/L) → Protein A affinity (capture, volume reduction, removes most impurities) → low-pH hold (pH <3.9, 60 min; viral inactivation) → anion exchange (DNA, HCP, endotoxin, leached Protein A) → cation exchange (product-related impurities: aggregates, truncated molecules) → nanofiltration 20–50 nm (viral safety) → UF/DF tangential filtration (concentration, buffer exchange) → 0.22 µm sterile filtration → formulation.澄清上清 → Protein A（捕获、减体积、去大部分杂质）→ 低 pH（病毒灭活）→ 阴离子交换（DNA、宿主蛋白、内毒素、残留 Protein A）→ 阳离子交换（聚体、截短体）→ 纳滤（病毒）→ UF/DF（浓缩换液）→ 除菌过滤 → 制剂。
讲解

各步目的可以直接用上面的括号。

Remicade 配方里各成分的作用★★★Turbica 练习Remicade excipients
参考答案 · Answer
  • Sucrose (saccharose): cryo/lyoprotectant and stabilizer (protects against aggregation during freeze-drying).蔗糖：冷冻/冻干保护剂，防聚集稳定剂。
  • Polysorbate 80: surfactant — prevents aggregation/adsorption at interfaces.聚山梨酯 80：表面活性剂，防止界面聚集/吸附。
  • Mono-/di-sodium phosphate: buffer, keeps pH (≈7).磷酸二氢钠/磷酸氢二钠：缓冲液，维持 pH。
讲解

联系第 2 讲 EPO：配方变化会影响免疫原性（聚山梨酯 80 与 PRCA）。

质控：每个图属于哪类？（鉴别/纯度/安全/活性）★★★Turbica 练习QC: classify each assay
参考答案 · Answer
  • Identity: peptide mapping (SB5 vs reference infliximab: each peak = a peptide; same peaks = same sequence), SDS-PAGE/native gel, glycan profile (2-AB labelled N-glycans, HILIC-UPLC after PNGase F).鉴别：肽图、SDS-PAGE/天然胶、糖谱。
  • Purity: SEC (HMW aggregates → monomer → LMW fragments), gels across purification steps (bands decrease), CE-SDS, charge variants.纯度：SEC、各纯化步骤的凝胶、CE-SDS、电荷变异。
  • Safety: sterility, endotoxin, mycoplasma, adventitious virus. Activity: SPR (ka, kd, KD = kd/ka), TNF-neutralization luciferase reporter (293-NF-κB-luc; IC50), in vivo mouse model with 3 groups (untreated, reference infliximab, new batch) reading circulating TNF-α.安全：无菌、内毒素、支原体、外源病毒。活性：SPR、TNF 中和报告基因、体内小鼠模型（3 组：未处理/参比品/新批次）。
讲解

肽图步骤：8 M 尿素变性 → DTT 还原二硫键 → 酶切成肽 → LC-MS。

glycan 步骤：PNGase F 酶切释放 N-聚糖 → 乙醇沉淀 → 2-AB 荧光标记 → HILIC-UPLC 分离 → 荧光检测（ex 330/em 420 nm）→ 定量半乳糖基化、去岩藻糖、高甘露糖、唾液酸化比例。

B.42025–26 真题：ozoralizumab (OZR)Anti-TNF nanobody · 8 questions

三价 VHH（两个抗 TNFα + 一个抗白蛋白）。8 个小问，共 10 分，1 小时。

题干 · Question paper（英文原文，图表未收录）

Exam subject for D2HP M2 students (10 points) Please write your answers clearly and concisely

Rheumatoid arthritis (RA) is an autoimmune disease characterised by chronic inflammation of the synovial tissue in the joints, which are gradually destroyed. In particular, the overproduction of TNF-α during the natural history of this disease has been shown to be harmful, leading to the development of a therapeutic arsenal targeting this cytokine, with different antibody formats. The latest biomolecule developed is ozoralizumab (OZR), shown in Figure 1, which was approved in Japan in 2022 for the treatment of RA. OZR is described below:

OZR is a nanobody next-generation anti-TNFα agent, consisting of a trivalent VHH (single variable heavy chain) structure composed of two anti-human TNFα VHHs and one anti-human serum albumin (HSA) VHH connected by two glycine-serine linkers consisting of nine amino acids. The anti-human TNFα VHH domain, as well as the anti-human of OZR were produced via the conventional phage-display technology using RNA isolated from llama-derived peripheral blood lymphocytes that had been immunized with human TNFα or HSA. A gene expression construct was generated by connecting two anti-TNFα VHHs and one anti-HSA- VHH via nine glycine-serine sequences. OZR is produced at the industrial level as a single- chain protein using recombinant genetic technology in Chinese hamster ovary cell lines. The molecular weight of OZR is 38,43 kDa.

Figure 1: Ozoralizumab (OZR)

Q1 Please draw a diagram of the expression construct that is transfected into the CHO cells.

The main steps for OZR purification consist on one affinity chromatography, followed by a cation exchange chromatography, and finally a size exclusion chromatography.

Q2 Please specify the role of each step. What other steps could complement this protocol?

Some non-clinical data are summarized below, compared to other anti-TNFα format molecules:

1) Table 1: Affinity for TNFα and albumin

OZRAdalimumab
Human TNFα20.2 pM127 pM
Mouse TNFα25 nM23 nM
HSA (human serum albumin)28 nM-
MSA (mouse serum albumin)110 nM-

Q3 Please describe and comment this results.

2) TNF-α neutralization in vitro. The assay is described below. Several constructs are tested, and compared with other anti-TNFα format molecules:

Anti-TNF bioassay. Anti-TNF VHH proteins were screened for their ability to counteract the cytotoxic effect of TNF on the mouse fibroblast fibrosarcoma cell line L929. The cells were grown to 90% confluency in Dulbecco’s modified Eagle’s medium supplemented with 10% fetal bovine serum, penicillin (100 units/ml), streptomycin sulfate (100 µg/ml), and L-glutamine (2 mM). The cells were plated in 96-well microtiter plates at a density of 5,000 cells/well and then incubated overnight. Serial dilutions of the camelid anti-TNF antibodies to be tested were mixed with a cytotoxic concentration of TNF (final assay concentration 0.5 ng/ml [15 IU/ml]). Actinomycin D was added to this mixture at a final concentration of 1 µg/ml to increase the sensitivity. After at least 30 minutes of incubation at 37°C, this mixture was added to the plated cells. Plates were incubated for 24 hours at 37°C in an atmosphere of 5% CO 2. Cell viability was determined using the tetrazolium salt WST-1. Dose-response curves and 50% maximum response concentrations (EC50) were calculated with GraphPad Prism software (GraphPad Software, San Diego, CA).

Commercially available anti-TNF antibodies etanercept, adalimumab, and infliximab were used as controls. Values are the mean or the mean SEM of duplicate measurements. Shown are the results for anti-TNF VHH alone (☐), (Anti-TNF VHH)x2 construct (▲), OZR (Anti-TNF VHH)x2_HSA construct (❍), etanercept (●), adalimumab (*), and infliximab (▽).

Fluorescence-labelled OZR was administered subcutaneously to collagen-induced arthritis (CIA) mouse models, and the fluorescence in serum, inflamed joints, and limbs was quantified and compared with that in the IgG-class adalimumab.

Q1画出转染 CHO 细胞的表达构建体Draw the expression construct transfected into CHO cells.
参考答案 · Answer
  • Promoter – (signal peptide) – anti-TNF VHH – linker – anti-HSA VHH – linker – anti-TNF VHH – stop – polyA. One ORF → one mRNA → one fusion protein.
  • Backbone: ori, selection marker.
  • Not the phage display step.
  • 启动子 –（信号肽）– 抗 TNF VHH – 连接肽 – 抗 HSA VHH – 连接肽 – 抗 TNF VHH – 终止密码子 – polyA。
  • 一个 ORF → 一条 mRNA → 一个融合蛋白。
  • 骨架：ori、筛选标记。
  • 不画噬菌体展示。
讲解

这是在问：你转进 CHO 细胞的那段 DNA 长什么样？OZR 是一条单链蛋白，所以只要一个基因（一个 ORF）。画一条线，从左到右：

启动子 → （信号肽）→ 抗 TNF VHH → linker → 抗白蛋白 VHH → linker → 抗 TNF VHH → 终止密码子 → polyA。

  • 启动子：启动转录；信号肽：让蛋白分泌出细胞；linker（Gly-Ser 9 aa）：柔性连接，让三个结构域各自折叠；polyA：让 mRNA 稳定。
  • 一个 ORF → 一条 mRNA → 一条融合蛋白。
  • 载体骨架还需要复制原点 (ori) 和筛选标记。
  • 噬菌体展示是找 VHH 的过程，不是这一步，别画进去。
Q2纯化每一步的作用；还可以补充哪些步骤
参考答案 · Answer
  • Q2 purification (role of each): affinity chromatography = capture + concentrate, removes most host-cell proteins/DNA (ozoralizumab has no Fc, so a Protein A–type or VHH-specific ligand is used, not classical Fc binding); cation exchange = removes charge variants, residual DNA/HCP, leached ligand; SEC = removes aggregates/fragments, polishing and buffer exchange. Complementary: viral inactivation (low pH/solvent-detergent) and nanofiltration, UF/DF, sterile filtration, plus QC (endotoxin, HCP, DNA).Q2 纯化（每步作用）：亲和层析 = 捕获并浓缩，去大部分宿主蛋白/DNA（OZR 没有 Fc，所以要用 Protein A 类或 VHH 专用配基，而不是靠 Fc 结合）；阳离子交换 = 去电荷变异体、残留 DNA/宿主蛋白、脱落配基；SEC = 去聚体/片段，精纯和换缓冲液。可补充：病毒灭活、纳滤、UF/DF、除菌过滤，以及内毒素/HCP/DNA 检测。
说明

ozoralizumab 的论文（Kyuuma 2023, Front Immunol）没有提供制造 / 纯化信息，所以第一步亲和层析具体用什么树脂没有核实。答“亲和层析 = 捕获步骤”即可，不要写死具体树脂；想补充可以说“VH3 家族的 VHH 能结合 Protein A（标准知识），也有 VHH 专用亲和配基”。

Q3描述并评论表 1（亲和力）Table 1 – interpret the affinities.
参考答案 · Answer
  • Human TNFα: OZR 20.2 pM vs adalimumab 127 pM → ~6× stronger.人 TNFα：OZR 20.2 pM vs 阿达木单抗 127 pM → 强约 6 倍。
  • Mouse TNFα: ~25 vs 23 nM, similar but ~1200× weaker than human → mouse model only partially reflects efficacy.小鼠 TNFα：约 25 vs 23 nM，相当，但比人弱约 1200 倍 → 小鼠模型只能部分反映药效。
  • Binds HSA 28 nM / MSA 110 nM (moderate: reversible "piggy-back")结合 HSA 28 nM / MSA 110 nM（中等：可逆"搭便车"）
  • adalimumab no albumin binding.阿达木单抗不结合白蛋白。
  • Reason: high-affinity VHH + 2 arms (avidity) — but adalimumab is bivalent too.原因：VHH 高亲和力 + 双臂（亲合力）——但阿达木单抗也是二价。
讲解

表 1 给了 OZR 和 adalimumab 对 TNF 的结合数据。读法：

  • 人 TNFα：OZR 20.2 pM，adalimumab 127 pM。数字小 = 结合紧，127 ÷ 20.2 ≈ 6，OZR 强约 6 倍。
  • 小鼠 TNFα：两者差不多（都在 nM 级），但比人 TNF 弱约 1200 倍 → 小鼠模型只能部分反映疗效（药在小鼠里没有在人里那么强）。
  • 白蛋白：OZR 结合人白蛋白 28 nM、小鼠白蛋白 110 nM，中等强度，可逆，像「搭便车」。adalimumab 不结合白蛋白。
  • 原因：高亲和力的 VHH，加上两只手同时抓（亲合力）。但注意 adalimumab 也是二价，所以不能把全部差别归因于「两只手」。

pM（皮摩尔）比 nM（纳摩尔）小 1000 倍，数一下单位。

Q4L929 中和实验：目的与回答的问题；描述并评论图 1Fig 1 – L929 TNF neutralization assay: principle and result.
参考答案 · Answer
  • L929 cells + actinomycin D are killed by TNFL929 + 放线菌素 D 被 TNF 杀伤
  • neutralizer → more live cells → higher WST-1 signal (OD405)中和剂越有效 → 活细胞越多 → WST-1 信号（OD405）越高
  • 24 h24 h
  • fit EC50.拟合 EC50。
  • Result: curve further left = stronger.结果：曲线越靠左越强。
  • Monovalent VHH weakest (~1 nM)单价 VHH 最弱（约 1 nM）
  • bivalent and OZR ~0.01 nM (~100×).二价和 OZR 约 0.01 nM（强约 100 倍）。
  • OZR ≈ bivalent without albumin arm → albumin arm does not impair neutralization.OZR ≈ 无白蛋白臂的二价 → 白蛋白臂不影响中和。
  • OZR ≈ etanercept/adalimumab, > infliximab.OZR ≈ 依那西普/阿达木单抗，优于英夫利西单抗。
  • Limits: mouse cells, duplicates, no stats.局限：小鼠细胞、重复 2 次、无统计。
讲解

L929 实验是在测中和 TNF 的功能：

  • L929 是小鼠成纤维细胞，加上放线菌素 D 后，TNF 会把它们杀死。
  • 加入能中和 TNF 的抗体 → TNF 被挡住 → 细胞活下来 → WST-1 显色（OD405）更高。
  • 24 h，拟合曲线得到 EC50。曲线越靠左 = 越少的药就能中和 = 越强。

结果：单价 VHH 最弱（约 1 nM）；二价和 OZR 约 0.01 nM（强约 100 倍）。OZR ≈ 二价 VHH，说明多加的抗白蛋白臂没有妨碍中和。OZR 与 etanercept、adalimumab 相当，比 infliximab 强。局限：小鼠细胞、重复少、没有统计检验。

Q5ADCC / CDC 实验：验证什么；分析结果并下结论（图 2）Fig 2 – ADCC and CDC: what is tested and what is concluded?
参考答案 · Answer
  • Tests Fc-mediated killing.测 Fc 介导的杀伤。
  • ADCC: infliximab ~70% > adalimumab ~50%ADCC：英夫利西单抗约 70% > 阿达木单抗约 50%
  • OZR ~5% = control IgG1.OZR 约 5% = 对照 IgG1。
  • CDC: adalimumab ~44%, infliximab ~32%, etanercept ~25%CDC：阿达木单抗约 44%、英夫利西单抗约 32%、依那西普约 25%
  • OZR ≈ 0.OZR ≈ 0。
  • Conclusion: OZR has no Fc → no ADCC/CDC → acts by neutralization only结论：OZR 无 Fc → 无 ADCC/CDC → 仅靠中和起效
  • possibly safer (hypothesis).可能更安全（假设）。
讲解

这张图测的是 Fc 介导的杀伤（ADCC 和 CDC）。比较对象：infliximab、adalimumab、etanercept、OZR 和对照 IgG1。

  • ADCC：infliximab 约 70% > adalimumab 约 50%；OZR 约 5%，和对照 IgG1 一样（等于没有）。
  • CDC：adalimumab 约 44%，infliximab 约 32%，etanercept 约 25%；OZR ≈ 0。

结论：OZR 没有 Fc → 没有 ADCC / CDC，只靠中和 TNF 起作用。可能更安全（因为不会杀掉膜上有 TNF 的正常细胞），但这是假设，不是证明。

Q6评论图 3（关节定位）Fig 3 – joint localization (Alexa680, SC, 8 h).
参考答案 · Answer
  • Joint fluorescence: adalimumab ~41 vs OZR ~74 → ~1.8× (**), n≈5–6.关节荧光：阿达木单抗约 41 vs OZR 约 74 → 约 1.8 倍（**），n≈5–6。
  • Why: ~38 kDa vs 150 kDa → better tissue penetration/retention原因：约 38 kDa vs 150 kDa → 穿透和滞留更好
  • flexible linkers.连接肽柔性。
  • Limits: one time point局限：单时间点
  • label ≠ intact drug标记 ≠ 完整药物
  • labeling ratio may differ标记比例可能不同
  • need serum and non-inflamed limb (joint/serum ratio)需血清和未发炎肢体数据（关节/血清比）
  • small n.样本量小。
讲解

这个实验看药到没到关节：用 Alexa680 荧光标记药物，皮下注射，8 小时后看关节处的荧光。

结果：adalimumab 约 41，OZR 约 74，比值约 1.8 倍，有统计学意义（**），每组 n≈5–6。

原因：OZR 只有约 38 kDa，IgG 是 150 kDa，小分子更容易穿透组织并留在关节里；柔性 linker 也有帮助。

局限：只有一个时间点；荧光不等于完整的药（可能是降解片段）；标记比例可能不同；需要同时看血清和没发炎的对侧肢体（关节/血清比）；样本少。

Q7评论表 2（半衰期）Table 2 – half-life.
参考答案 · Answer
  • Monovalent 0.75 h → bivalent 1.6 h (~2×) → OZR 39 h (~24× vs bivalent, ~52× vs monovalent).单价 0.75 h → 二价 1.6 h（约 2 倍）→ OZR 39 h（比二价约 24 倍，比单价约 52 倍）。
  • Small proteins are filtered by kidney (< ~60 kDa)小蛋白被肾滤过（< 约 60 kDa）
  • anti-HSA arm binds albumin → size above cutoff + albumin recycling via FcRn.抗 HSA 臂结合白蛋白 → 体积超过肾阈值 + 白蛋白经 FcRn 回收。
  • Missing: adalimumab t½ for direct comparison (IgG ~480 h).缺：阿达木单抗半衰期做直接对比（IgG 约 480 h）。
讲解

表 2 的半衰期（t½）一步一步看：

  • 单价 VHH：0.75 h。
  • 二价：1.6 h（约 2 倍，分子变大一点）。
  • OZR：39 h（比二价长约 24 倍，比单价长约 52 倍）。

为什么：小于约 60 kDa 的蛋白会被肾脏滤掉，所以单价和二价都很短。OZR 里的抗白蛋白臂结合白蛋白，等于搭了白蛋白的便车：分子变大不被肾脏滤掉，同时白蛋白被 FcRn 回收，所以长得多。

缺什么：没有 adalimumab 的 t½ 做直接对比（IgG 约 480 h），所以不能说 OZR 比 IgG 长。

Q8与完整抗体（如阿达木单抗）相比，OZR 的利弊Advantages and drawbacks of OZR vs a full antibody.
参考答案 · Answer
  • + higher affinity (6×), equal potency
  • + better joint penetration (1.8×)
  • + t½ 0.75 → 39 h via albumin without Fc
  • + no ADCC/CDC
  • + simple single chain. − immunogenicity unknown (camelid VHH; aggregates)
  • − t½ still shorter than IgG
  • − weak on mouse TNF → limited model
  • − albumin-binding side effects unknown
  • − no Fc/FcRn route.
  • + 亲和力高 6 倍、效力相当
  • + 关节穿透更好（1.8 倍）
  • + 靠白蛋白而非 Fc 把半衰期从 0.75 h 延到 39 h
  • + 无 ADCC/CDC
  • + 单链结构简单。
  • − 免疫原性未知（羊驼 VHH；聚集）
  • − 半衰期仍短于 IgG
  • − 对小鼠 TNF 弱 → 模型有限
  • − 白蛋白结合的副作用未知
  • − 没有 Fc/FcRn 途径。
讲解

这是一道「利弊比较」题，用 D0 里的模板：先写量化的优点，再写缺点，最后一句判断。

优点：亲和力强 6 倍、效力相当；关节穿透好 1.8 倍；半衰期从 0.75 h 延到 39 h，而且不需要 Fc；没有 ADCC/CDC；单链结构简单。

缺点：免疫原性未知（骆驼来源的 VHH，还有聚体问题）；半衰期仍短于 IgG；对小鼠 TNF 很弱，所以动物模型有限；结合白蛋白的副作用未知；没有 Fc，没有 FcRn 回收途径。

结尾加一句：值得临床使用，但免疫原性需要临床确认。

B.52024–25 真题：RSV / nirsevimabAnti-RSV F antibody · 5 questions

nirsevimab（YTE 改造的全人源抗 RSV F 抗体）对比 palivizumab。5 个小问，共 10 分。

题干 · Question paper（英文原文，图表未收录）

2024-1025 Exam subject for D2HP M2 students (10 points)

Please write your answers clearly and concisely

Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of hospitalization and infant mortality worldwide. There are currently no approved vaccines against RSV. Immunoprophylaxis is usually carried on with the monoclonal antibody palivizumab which is a humanized antibody. However its use is limited to extremely vulnerable infants in resource-rich settings due to its high cost and the need for monthly injections throughout the RSV season. Nirsevimab (formerly MEDI8897) is a novel human recombinant mAb that recently received approval for the prevention of RSV infection in newborns and infants during their first RSV season.

Q1 Please explain what are the principal differences between a humanized and a human monoclonal therapeutic antibody. Reply within 5-8 lines.

Q2 what are the different functionalities excepted for an anti-viral antibody. Please explain what part of the antibody is implicated for each functionality.

Nirsevimab targets the RSV envelope surface protein F, which is responsible of the fusion of the viral envelope with the target cell. It was derived from a first human antibody named MEDI8897* which was engineered to increase the affinity for target protein F. The Fc fragment of MEDI8897* was then engineered by the introduction of 3 amino acids substitution on the Fc part of the protein, to give nirsevimab (MEDI8897). The biological activity of nirsevimab was studied by different experiments.

A first in vitro test was done on target cells infected with 2 different RSV strains. The assay description is given below: Each monoclonal antibody was serially diluted in 96-well plates prior to the addition of a fixed concentration of RSV A2 or RSV 9320 at a median tissue culture infectious dose (TCID50) of 500 per well and was incubated for 1 hour followed by the addition of HEp-2 cells. After 5 days of incubation at 37°C, RSV-infected cells were fixed and viral replication was measured by ELISA, using an anti-RSV antibody (Millipore, Cat# MAB8262 Clone 133-1H at 1:5000 dilution) and a second detection antibody (Dako polyclonal goat anti-mouse IgG-HRP Cat# P0447 at 1:4000 dilution). The OD450nm was measured using a microplate reader. Half- maximal inhibitory concentrations (IC50) were calculated using 4PL nonlinear curve fitting on the GraphPad Prism. Results are given in Table 1:

Table 1: Comparison of the antiviral activities of MEDI8897* and MEDI8897 (nirsevimab) in vitro. The activity of MEDI8897* and MEDI8897 (nirsevimab) was determined by measuring the expression of RSV F protein in RSV infected cells using an ELISA assay. IC50 values were calculated

Monoclonal antibodyRSV A2 IC50 (ng/mL)RSV B9320 IC50 (ng/mL)
MEDI88971.6011.807
MEDI8897*1.6592.054
Palivizumab200.9155.9

and represent the concentration of mAb required for a 50% reduction in absorbance measured at 450 nm.

Q3 What functionality is tested with this assay? Please comment the table and give a conclusion.

Fc fragment activity was also tested, though 2 experiments that are summarized below:

a) Table 2: Comparison of anti-RSV F protein Fc receptor binding by surface plasmon resonance

mAbFcγRI binding affinity (nM)
Nirsevimab10,3

Fc receptor is expressed on immune cells that have cytotoxic activity.

b) Antibody-mediated phagocytosis tests (ADCP) show comparable activity between nirsevimab and palivizumab, and greater than that mediated by a non-relevant antibody taken as a negative control; whereas antibody-mediated cytotoxicity tests (ADCC) show less activity of nirservimab compared to palivizumab, but greater than that mediated by a non-relevant antibody taken as a negative control.

Q4 Please comment this results. Considering the overall experiments, could you state the principal biological activity of nirsevimab?

Finally, pre-clinical tests were carried out on monkeys, summarized below: Niversimab (MEDI88997) was then tested in a Good Laboratory Practice (GLP)–compliant nonclinical repeat-dose toxicology study in which cynomolgus monkeys received five once-weekly intramuscular (300 mg, fixed dose) or intravenous (300 mg/kg, body weight–scaled) administrations of mAb. Results showed no local or systemic adverse effects through day 31 (terminal necropsy) nor through day 169 after a 5-month dose-free recovery phase. The concentrations of MEDI8897 in monkey serum samples were determined for PK assessment. MEDI8897 exhibited linear and dose-proportional PK, with a mean t1/2 calculated to be 40.5 or 39.9 days after intravenous or intramuscular dosing, respectively. As expected, the serum t1/2 for MEDI8897 in cynomolgus monkeys was three- to fourfold longer than that determined for palivizumab.

Q5 Regarding this experiment, could you explain in which way nirsevimab is better adapted than palivizumab for a prophylactic treatment against RSV infection in infants?

Q1人源化 vs 全人源（5–8 行）
参考答案 · Answer
  • 人源化抗体（后缀 -zumab，如 palivizumab）：来源于小鼠杂交瘤，把鼠源 CDR（或只是 SDR）移植到人的框架区和恒定区上；仍保留少量鼠源序列，可能产生抗药抗体（对 CDR/独特型），有时需要框架区回复突变以保住亲和力。
  • 全人源抗体（-mumab，如 nirsevimab）：整条序列都是人的，来源是噬菌体展示的人库、人类 Ig 转基因小鼠或单个人 B 细胞，不需要人源化这一步。免疫原性预期更低，但不是零（例如阿达木单抗的 ADA 发生率仍有 21–57%）。
  • 后续优化：全人源抗体依然可以体外亲和力成熟和 Fc 改造，本题 nirsevimab 就是这样得到的。
Q2抗病毒抗体的功能与对应结构
参考答案 · Answer
  1. 中和病毒（阻止 F 蛋白介导的融合/入侵）：Fab 的互补位（CDR）。
  2. ADCC（NK 细胞经 FcγRIIIa 杀伤感染细胞）、ADCP（巨噬细胞吞噬）、CDC（C1q 结合激活补体）：Fc 段。
  3. 长半衰期、持续保护：Fc 与 FcRn 结合（回收）。
  4. 调理作用、聚集病毒、促进免疫复合物清除：Fab + Fc 共同作用。
Q3中和实验与表 1
参考答案 · Answer
  • 测的是体外中和（抗病毒）效价：抗体连续稀释，与固定量 RSV（A2 或 B9320）孵育 1 h，加入 HEp-2 细胞，培养 5 天，用抗 RSV 抗体做 ELISA（OD450）测病毒复制；IC50 = 抑制 50% 所需浓度，越小越强。
  • 数据：nirsevimab IC50 为 1.60（A2）和 1.81（B）ng/mL；MEDI8897* 为 1.66 和 2.05，几乎相同；palivizumab 为 200.9 和 155.9。
  • 倍数：palivizumab 比 nirsevimab 弱约 125 倍（A2）和 86 倍（B）。
  • 结论：nirsevimab 对两个亚型都有很强的中和力，远强于 palivizumab（亲和力工程的结果）。nirsevimab 与 MEDI8897* 的 IC50 一致，说明 Fc 上的三个氨基酸替换不影响 Fab 的中和。
  • 局限：体外实验，只测了两个毒株，未涉及耐药突变。
Q4FcγRI 结合与效应功能；nirsevimab 的主要活性
参考答案 · Answer
  • 表 2 是 KD（nM），数值越大结合越弱：nirsevimab 10.3 nM，MEDI8897* 1.24 nM，palivizumab 1.01 nM。nirsevimab 对 FcγRI 的结合约比亲本弱 8 倍（比 palivizumab 弱约 10 倍）。
  • 功能：ADCP 与 palivizumab 相当，高于阴性对照；ADCC 低于 palivizumab，但高于阴性对照 → 效应功能被减弱，但没有完全消失。
  • 三个氨基酸替换其实就是 YTE（M252Y/S254T/T256E），它的设计目的是增强 FcRn 结合，延长半衰期，副作用是 FcγR 结合略降。
  • 结论：nirsevimab 的主要生物活性是强力中和病毒（Q3），效应功能不是主要机制，只保留一部分。减弱效应功能可能有利于降低免疫病理风险，这是推测，课上没有讲。
Q5为什么比 palivizumab 更适合婴儿预防
参考答案 · Answer
  • 半衰期：猴子中约 40 天，比 palivizumab 长 3–4 倍（YTE 增强 FcRn 回收）。一次肌注就可能覆盖整个 RSV 季（约 5 个月），不需要每月注射，依从性好，减少就诊，成本更低，适用于资源有限地区。
  • 效价高约 100 倍：所需剂量更小。
  • 安全性：GLP 毒理，没有局部或全身不良反应，5 个月恢复期也无迟发毒性；PK 线性、与剂量成正比，便于固定剂量给药。
  • 全人源，免疫原性风险较低。
  • 局限：这是猴子数据，婴儿的 PK 和安全性需要临床确认。

B.62023–24 真题：四价双特异性抗体 Bs4Ab-VATetravalent bispecific antibody · 6 questions

抗 VEGF Fab + 抗 Ang2 scFv 的 IgG 样双特异分子。6 个小问，共 10 分。

题干 · Question paper（英文原文，图表未收录）

2023-2024 Exam subject for D2HP M2 students (10 points)

Please write your answers clearly and concisely

Antibody engineering has permitted bispecific antibodies (BsAb) conception and development. In this publication, the authors describe the design and characterization of a tetravalent bispecific antibody (Bs4Ab), shown schematically in Figure 1.

Figure 1: Schematic representation of Bs4Ab. VEGF: vascular endothelial growth factor. A therapy with drugs such as bevacizumab (Avastin®) is widely used as an angiogenesis inhibitor for the treatment of colorectal cancer. Angiopoietin-2 (Ang2) expression has been shown to function as a key regulator of tumor angiogenesis and metastasis. Ang-2 is up-regulated in many cancer types and correlated with poor prognosis. LCO6 is a fully human antibody that neutralizes specifically the binding of Ang-2 to its receptor Tie2, and showed potent anti-tumoral and anti-angiogenic efficacy.

Q1 Please explain the words tetravalent bispecific antibody in this context (1 point)

Q2 Considering that bevacizumab and LCO6 antibodies are available, please explain the steps you would take to build a host cell that could produce Bs4Ab (5-6 lines max).(2 points)

The production of Bs4Ab-VA is explained hereafter: The transient expression levels of Bs4Ab-VA in human embryonic kidney (HEK)293 cells was 150 mg/L, which is similar to that of the parental anti-VEGF and anti-Ang2 antibodies. The monomeric content after protein A purification was 97% and 90% for the two parental antibodies and Bs4Ab-VA, respectively. Aggregates in Bs4Ab-VA were removed using ceramic hydroxyapatite chromatography (CHT).

Q3 Justify the use of this producing cell. As the purification process seems pretty efficient, what supplemental step(s) would you include for a production at the industrial level? Please justify.(2 points)

A first result of in vitro functional control of Bs4Ab is given in Figure 2:

Figure 2: Binding of Bs4Ab-VA, anti-VEGF and anti-Ang2 to VEGF and Ang2.

Q5 Please explain the steps of these experiments (you can do some schematic draws) and comment the results. (2 points) ➔ the inhibitory functionality of the bispecific Bs5Ab-VA is comparable to the parental antibodies

Pre-clinical studies are performed with a mouse animal model. The results are reported on Figures 4 and 5.

Q6 a. What will be the distribution of Bs4Ab-VA in the body after intraveinous administration? What are the main expected elimination steps for this antibody? b. Please analyse and comment the figures.(2 points)

Figure 4. Pharmacokinetics of Bs4Ab-VA, anti-VEGF and anti-Ang2 antibodies in non-tumor bearing mice. Serum PK of Bs4Ab-VA (red line), anti-VEGF (blue line) and anti-Ang2 (green line) after intravenous administration in non-tumor bearing mice.

Figure 5. Tumor growth inhibition of Bs4Ab-VA, anti-VEGF and anti-Ang2, and their combination using a colon cancer (Colo205) mouse xenograft model. When the tumor reached a volume of approximately 200 mm3, mice (n D 6) were administered intraperitoneally twice per week for four weeks with 14 mg/kg of Bs4Ab-VA, 10 mg/kg of anti-VEGF, anti-Ang2, their combination, and a non- binding isotype antibody. Untreated mice were used as control. 10 mg/kg of each of the two parental antibodies were used for the combination study.

Q1"四价双特异性"（1 分）
参考答案 · Answer

一个分子有 4 个抗原结合位点（四价），识别 2 种不同抗原（双特异：VEGF 和 Ang2）。结构是 IgG1 样：两条臂各有一个 bevacizumab 的 Fab（抗 VEGF），在 Fab 与 Fc 之间的铰链区各接一个 scFv（LCO6，抗 Ang2），所以一个分子可以同时结合 2 个 VEGF 和 2 个 Ang2。

Q2构建宿主细胞的步骤（5–6 行）
参考答案 · Answer
  1. 取 bevacizumab 和 LCO6 的 VH/VL 序列（已有抗体，基因合成）。
  2. 设计两个基因：重链融合基因 = bevacizumab VH-CH1 – 柔性连接肽 – LCO6 scFv（VH–(G4S)4–VL）– 连接肽 – 铰链-CH2-CH3；轻链 = bevacizumab VL-CL。加信号肽，密码子优化。
  3. 克隆进哺乳动物表达载体（启动子、polyA、筛选标记、复制起点），重链和轻链可在同一质粒或两个质粒上。
  4. 转染宿主细胞（HEK293 瞬时，或 CHO 稳定）。
  5. 筛选高表达克隆（ELISA 测滴度），建立主细胞库和工作细胞库。
Q3为什么用 HEK293；工业化还要补什么
参考答案 · Answer
  • 用 HEK293：人源哺乳动物细胞，能正确折叠这种多结构域分子并形成二硫键，能糖基化 Fc（N297，影响 Fc 功能和免疫原性），分泌表达；瞬时表达快，适合研究阶段，题中 150 mg/L 与亲本抗体相当。细菌不能糖基化，酵母的糖型不是人类型。
  • 工业化：换成稳定的 CHO 细胞系（HEK293 瞬时转染难以放大和满足 GMP，要有细胞库和稳定性）。
  • 补充纯化步骤：Protein A 之后只有 90% 单体，聚集体问题更突出，已经用 CHT 去除。另外要加 低 pH 病毒灭活 + 病毒过滤（题目没有）、离子交换精纯（去 HCP、DNA、内毒素）、UF/DF（浓缩并换成制剂缓冲液）、无菌过滤，以及完整的质控（聚集、电荷变异体、糖型、效价）。
Q4图 2：ELISA 结合
参考答案 · Answer
  • 回答的问题：Bs4Ab-VA 是否仍然特异地结合两个靶点，结合力与亲本抗体相比如何？
  • 图 a（VEGF 包被）：Bs4Ab EC50 3.04 nM，anti-VEGF 2.41 nM，anti-Ang2 不结合（特异性对照）。图 b（Ang2 包被）：Bs4Ab 3.66 nM，anti-Ang2 0.594 nM，anti-VEGF 不结合。
  • 结论：双特异分子保留了对两个抗原的特异结合。对 VEGF 的结合与亲本几乎相同，对 Ang2 约弱 6 倍，可能因为 scFv 是单价且构象受限（亲合力低于完整 IgG）。
  • 局限：ELISA 分别用两个抗原，不能证明同时结合两个靶点，需要夹心 ELISA 或 SPR。
Q5图 3：受体磷酸化实验
参考答案 · Answer
  • 步骤：用过表达 Tie2（Ad293）或 VEGFR2（HEK293）的细胞 → 配体（Ang2 或 VEGF165）与递增浓度的抗体预先混合 → 加到细胞上 → 配体结合引起受体二聚化和自磷酸化 → 裂解细胞，用 ELISA 之类定量磷酸化受体 → 以 % 磷酸化对抗体浓度作图。同型 IgG1 是阴性对照。
  • 结果：Tie2：Bs4Ab IC50 1.37 nM，anti-Ang2 1.24 nM。VEGFR2：Bs4Ab 4.32 nM，anti-VEGF 4.78 nM。两者几乎相同，高浓度时磷酸化降到约 10% 以下；同型对照基本不抑制。
  • 结论：双特异抗体功能性中和两个配体的能力与亲本抗体相当，作用是特异的。
Q6a静脉给药后的分布与消除
参考答案 · Answer
  • 分布：约 200 kDa 的大分子，初期主要在血浆/血管内，分布容积小；通过对流和 FcRn 介导的转胞吞缓慢进入间质，到达肿瘤时与 VEGF、Ang2 结合。
  • 消除：太大，不经肾小球滤过；主要是蛋白水解代谢（内皮细胞、网状内皮系统的溶酶体，FcRn 回收可保护一部分）、靶点介导清除（与 VEGF/Ang2 结合后内化）、免疫复合物清除。
Q6b图 4 与图 5
参考答案 · Answer
  • 图 4（非荷瘤小鼠 PK，对数坐标）：双相下降，先快速分布，再缓慢消除；Bs4Ab-VA（红）在早期浓度高于两个亲本抗体，之后三条曲线接近。说明增加 scFv 后 PK 没有变差，Fc 仍保留 FcRn 的功能。（图分辨率低，“后期接近”的判断不确定。）
  • 图 5（Colo205 异种移植）：第 29 天肿瘤体积约为：未处理 1350、对照 IgG 1080、anti-VEGF 约 630、anti-Ang2 约 570、两者联用约 450 mm³，Bs4Ab-VA 最低，约 340 mm³。
    • 同型 IgG 对照略有作用，说明需要用它而不是只用未处理组。
    • Bs4Ab 14 mg/kg 与每个亲本 10 mg/kg 大致等摩尔（按约 205 kDa vs 150 kDa 估算），联用组等于给了两倍剂量，但 Bs4Ab 仍然最好。
    • 结论：同时抑制 VEGF 和 Ang2 比单靶点更好，一个分子的效果不低于两个抗体联用，而且生产、监管上只需要一个产品。
    • 局限：n=6，单一模型，4 周，没有毒性和免疫原性数据。

B.7早期真题：ranibizumab 与 bevacizumabEarly paper (year not stated) · Exercise 1

卷子没有标年份，早于 2023–24。同一份卷子的 Exercise 2 是 miR-181c，与 B.1 的练习 1 逐字相同，答案见 B.1。

题干 · Question paper（英文原文，图表未收录）

Exercise 1: approximate time: 1h (10 points total) Ranibizumab (Lucentis®) is a recombinant humanised monoclonal antibody Fab fragment directed against human vascular endothelial growth factor-A (VEGF-A). It binds with high affinity to VEGF-A isoforms, thereby preventing the binding of VEGF-A to its receptors VEGFR- 1 and VEGFR-2. Binding of VEGF-A to its receptors induces endothelial cell proliferation and neo-vascularisation as well as vascular permeability, all of which are thought to contribute to the progression of the neo-vascular form of age-related macular degeneration (AMD). This antibody fragment is produced by Genentech, in E. coli, using recombinant DNA technology. Ranibizumab is derived from bevacizumab (Avastin®), a full-length antibody that also targets VEGF and is used for the treatment of colon cancer, to control neovascularisation in metastatic solid tumours. Bevacizumab is also produced by Genentech, in CHO cells. Answer the following questions concisely (max 6 lines per answer).

1) Justify the molecular format chosen for ranibizumab compared to bevacizumab. Explain why different producing cells are used for ranibizumab and bevacizumab production (2 pts).

2) Describe the steps involved in obtaining the ranibizumab producing cells, given the origin of this antibody (2 pts).

3) What is the mechanism of action of ranibizumab and bevacizumab? Comment on the following comparative data. How do you think ranibizumab improvement has been achieved? (2pts)

Target: VEGF-A

ranibizumabbevacizumab
Affinity (kD; pM)9.2 ; 17958 ; 4456
Potency (IC50; pM)88 ; 1140500 ; 1476

Because ranibizumab is highly effective, but considerably expensive, bevacizumab, which costs approximately one-fortieth the price of ranibizumab, is now used for ADM treatment.

4) Could you outline the potential advantages and disadvantages of using bevacizumab compared to ranibizumab? (2 pts)

The efficacy of ranibizumab and bevacizumab was compared in an in vitro model of retina epithelium cultures. In brief, on the second day of cultivation, retina epithelium cultures were exposed to bevacizumab or ranibizumab at indicated concentrations. The flow-through of the growth medium was collected at 6 h and the VEGF content was measured by a VEGF-ELISA following the manufacturer’s instructions. The results are show in Figure 1.

Figure 1: Minimal concentration of VEGF inhibition. Organ cultures were stimulated with VEGF-antagonist and VEGF content of the supernatant was detected after 6 h. Significance was determined by Student's t test. “*” p<0.05; “**” p<0.01, “***” p<0.001

5) Please describe the figure and conclude about the results (2pt).

Q1格式与细胞
参考答案 · Answer
  • ranibizumab 是 Fab 片段（约 48 kDa），经玻璃体内注射治疗 AMD。体积小，更容易穿过视网膜到达靶点；离开眼睛后在全身很快清除，减少全身性 VEGF 抑制；没有 Fc，所以没有 ADCC/CDC，也不引起眼内补体炎症。bevacizumab 是全长 IgG1，用于癌症（抑制肿瘤新生血管），Fc 的效应功能和长半衰期是优势。
  • 细胞：Fab 没有 Fc，不需要 N-糖基化，结构简单，可以在大肠杆菌周质分泌并形成二硫键，便宜、快、产量高。全长 IgG 需要糖基化和多结构域折叠，要用哺乳动物细胞（CHO）。
Q2获得 ranibizumab 生产细胞的步骤（原点是 bevacizumab）
参考答案 · Answer

bevacizumab 本身来源于小鼠抗 VEGF 抗体（杂交瘤）经人源化而成。所以：取 bevacizumab 的人源化序列 → 只保留 VH-CH1 和 VL-CL（Fab）→ 亲和力成熟（噬菌体展示，CDR 引入突变，对 VEGF 淘选）→ 把选出的 VH-CH1、VL-CL 基因克隆进大肠杆菌表达载体（信号肽、启动子、抗性标记）→ 转化大肠杆菌 → 筛选高产克隆 → 建立细胞库。

Q3作用机制与数据
参考答案 · Answer
  • 两者都中和 VEGF-A，阻止其结合 VEGFR-1/2，从而抑制内皮细胞增殖、新生血管形成和血管通透性。
  • 数据：KD 越小越强。ranibizumab 9.2 vs 58 pM（强约 6 倍）；第二组 179 vs 4456 pM（强约 25 倍）。IC50 88 vs 500 pM（强约 6 倍）；1140 vs 1476 pM（只强约 1.3 倍）。
  • 表里每格有两个数（应是两种 VEGF-A 亚型或两种条件，题目没说明，答题时说“两组数据”即可）。
  • 改进方式：亲和力成熟。在 CDR（可含少量框架区）引入突变，做突变体文库，噬菌体展示把突变体展示在噬菌体表面，对 VEGF 反复淘选，选出结合更强的，同时检查突变后其他性质（特异性、稳定性、效价）有没有受损。批注里的"introduce many mutations"和"check previous properties"就是这个意思。
Q4用 bevacizumab 的利弊
参考答案 · Answer
  • 优势：价格约为 1/40，可及性高；全长 IgG 有长半衰期，注射间隔可能更长；已有大量肿瘤使用经验。
  • 不足：亲和力、效价低；分子大，视网膜穿透较差；有 Fc，可能引起补体激活和免疫反应；进入血液后清除慢，可能造成全身性 VEGF 抑制；用于 AMD 属于超适应症，需要从大瓶分装，存在污染、聚集和剂量不均的风险。
Q5图 1：视网膜上皮培养物的 VEGF 中和
参考答案 · Answer
  • 实验：视网膜上皮培养物自己会分泌 VEGF；培养第二天加入不同浓度的抗体，6 h 后收集上清，用 VEGF-ELISA 定量剩余 VEGF（pg/mL）。图有 B（ranibizumab）和 C（bevacizumab）两张，均以不加抗体为对照，用 Student's t 检验。
  • 结果：两种抗体在高浓度下都能把上清中的 VEGF 降到接近零；随着浓度降低，抑制逐渐消失。显著性差异标注（*、**、***）集中在较高的浓度。
  • 结论：两者都有效，呈浓度依赖。
  • 局限：横轴是 µg/mL 而不是摩尔浓度，Fab（约 48 kDa）和全长 IgG（约 150 kDa）每微克含的分子数不同，不能直接比较效力（手写批注里的 “they are different doses” 正是这个）。对照组 VEGF 水平和误差棒很大。
附 C

速查数字Numbers to remember

“一个观点一个数字”

C.1一页速查：必记数字

一页速查：必记数字★★★汇总Numbers to remember
速记 · Quick reference
  • IgG t½ ≈ 21 days (480 h); IgG3 144 h; nirsevimab (YTE) ≈ 69 days; kidney cutoff ≈ 60 kDa; mAb 150 kDa; Fab ~50; scFv ~25–30; VHH 15 kDa; BiTE 50 kDa, t½ 2 h.IgG 半衰期约 3 周；IgG3 144 h；nirsevimab 约 69 天；肾滤过阈值约 60 kDa；mAb 150 kDa；VHH 15 kDa；BiTE 50 kDa/2 h。
  • ADA: negligible <2%, tolerable 2–15%, marked >15%; adalimumab 21–57%; infliximab 6–53%; EPO PRCA 18 → 0.3 per 100,000.ADA 分级与数字；EPO PRCA 病例下降。
  • LNP molar ratios: ionizable 30–50%, cholesterol 30–50%, helper 10–30%, PEG <2%; ionizable lipid pKa 6.2–6.5; purification ≥400% ↑ expression.LNP 摩尔比；pKa；纯化后表达 ↑ ≥400%。
  • miRNA 20–25 nt, seed nt 2–8, ~7 nt site in 3′UTR; siRNA 21–23 nt. Cas9 PAM NGG, guide ~20 nt, cut ~3 bp upstream of PAM. EV 30–1000 nm.miRNA 20–25 nt；种子 2–8；siRNA 21–23 nt；Cas9 PAM NGG；EV 30–1000 nm。
